3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **scripts/run-all.mjs** (CREATED — M.W1 orchestrator, the keystone):
     The concurrency-limited report-all orchestrator. Reads tier membership from package.json, fans every leaf out across a worker pool, collects ALL exit codes, names every failed gate, exits 1 iff any red.
     ```js
     import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
     import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
     import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
     import { cpus } from "node:os";
     const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
     const flag = (name, def) => { const a = argv.find((x) => x.startsWith(`--${name}=`)); return a ? a.slice(name.length + 3) : def; };
     const workers = Math.max(1, Number(flag("workers", Math.max(1, Math.min(8, cpus().length - 2)))) || 1);
     const pkgPath = resolve(flag("pkg", "package.json"));
     const cwd = dirname(pkgPath);
     const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(pkgPath, "utf8"));
     const tiers = argv.includes("--all") ? ["proof:correctness", "proof:hygiene"] : argv.filter((a) => a.startsWith("--tier=")).map((a) => a.slice("--tier=".length));
     // ... parses each tier's && chain into leaves; worker pool runs `sh -c leaf`; summary prints "RUN-ALL FAILED: <leaf>" + "RUN-ALL ALL GREEN"; process.exit(1 iff any failed)
     ```
     Uses cursor-based worker pool; `spawn("sh", ["-c", leaf], { cwd, stdio: ["ignore", "inherit", "inherit"], env })`.

   - **scripts/proof-report-all.mjs** (CREATED — M.W1 born-RED gate):
     Plants a real multi-red tree {g1✓ g2✗ g3✓ g4✗} in a temp sandbox, runs the REAL run-all.mjs. 6 clauses: C1 (both g2+g4 surfaced one pass, exit 1), C2 (serial `&&` aborts-first — clears .ran markers first), C3 (clean parity exit 0), C4 (no-swallow, red exits 1), C5 (concurrency: workers=2 overlaps sleepers, workers=1 serial), C6 (proof:gate-is-runtime + proof:ci-coverage still green). Key helpers: `leaf(name, code)` writes a node script writing a `.ran` marker; `sleeper(name, ms)` timestamps spawn+exit; `ts(n)` robust (returns null if missing); `overlaps(a,b)` checks interval overlap. GATE TIER: hygiene.

   - **package.json** (MODIFIED):
